Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the post-quantum object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 710. The moduli space has dimension at most 784 with 3 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the post-quantum decomposition. By minimality, the element a^710 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 784-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
